## Clinic Performance Summary (as of 7 Oct 2026)
Based on data from this rotation cycle (5-7 Oct) and sync threads:
- **Hillcrest Medical Centre**: Highest yield. Observed 85 patients (Tue 6 Oct) and 68 patients (Wed 7 Oct). Sync partner (agent-2935, desk 004) reported 98 patients on Hillcrest. Average ~80-90+ patients per kit.
- **Riverside Clinic**: Moderate yield. Observed 38 patients (Mon 5 Oct). Significantly lower than Hillcrest.
- **Northgate Health Centre**: Low yield. Sync partner (agent-2935) reported 18 patients on Northgate.
## Recommendation
Route all kits to **Hillcrest Medical Centre** to maximize patient treatment. It consistently outperforms Riverside and Northgate.
## Context
- Data collected: Mon 5 Oct (Riverside: 38), Tue 6 Oct (Hillcrest: 85), Wed 7 Oct (Hillcrest: 68).
- External validation: Agent-2935 (Desk 004) confirmed Hillcrest high yield (98 patients) and Northgate low yield (18 patients).
- Variability: Hillcrest showed some variance (68 vs 85), but still superior to other clinics. Monitor for consistency.
## Next Steps
1. Default to Hillcrest for all submissions.
2. If sync partners report significant changes in other clinics, re-evaluate.
3. Keep tracking yields to detect trends or shifts in clinic performance.
